## Recorded Candidates

### `online-feature-prior-bce` (`FeaturePrior-BCAP`)
- **Changed factors**: ParticleGAN 0.9.6 public bcap_adam trainer preset, Shared 64-frame FIFO memory, seven replay samples and velocity context, Global non-saturating loss and cosine schedule (start=0.5, floor=0.05), Explicit replay_policy=none: train every arriving observation once, batch_size=1, Predict before arrival; learn next-frame targets from the preceding detached context, Causal state reader queries detached associative memory and carries current image, velocity, salient history and bounded repetition age in the existing 16-dimensional context, New observed-feature conditional prior: fixed named deterministic orthogonal 8x64 encoder; at most 32 latent feature means and 64 observed-context outcome counts; learn statistics only after successful current-observation updates, Freeze storage prior; learned additive conditional transform preserves each sampled MoG perturbation and task capacity, samples empirical feature quantiles at nearest past context; no image output bypass or stored-frame replay, G-phase posterior encodes only the current arrived frame; BCE reconstruction weight 10 trains the unchanged image decoder; D and serving use learned conditional prior without current target or generator probes, Ablate associative-history context term; retain appearance, velocity, salient content and repetition age

### `online-gail-bcap-bce` (`GAIL-BCAP`)
- **Changed factors**: ParticleGAN 0.9.6 public bcap_adam trainer preset, Shared 64-frame FIFO memory, seven replay samples and velocity context, Global non-saturating loss and cosine schedule (start=0.5, floor=0.05), Explicit replay_policy=none: train every arriving observation once, batch_size=1, Predict before arrival; learn next-frame targets from the preceding detached context, Causal state reader queries detached associative memory and carries current image, velocity, salient history and bounded repetition age in the existing 16-dimensional context, New observed-feature conditional prior: fixed named deterministic orthogonal 8x64 encoder; at most 32 latent feature means and 64 observed-context outcome counts; learn statistics only after successful current-observation updates, Freeze storage prior; learned additive conditional transform preserves each sampled MoG perturbation and task capacity, samples empirical feature quantiles at nearest past context; no image output bypass or stored-frame replay, G-phase posterior encodes only the current arrived frame; BCE reconstruction weight 10 trains the unchanged image decoder; D and serving use learned conditional prior without current target or generator probes, Ablate associative-history context term; retain appearance, velocity, salient content and repetition age, Switch critic adversarial objective to Hinge loss paired with BCAP input-gradient cap and write_lr=0.5 causal-state fast-weight updates, Stabilized GAIL-BCAP generator objective: cancel unbounded linear Hinge generator slope and optimize prioritized BCE survival anchor minus bounded sigmoid critic style bonus (weight 0.1) modulated by causal discounted return advantage (gamma=0.99, scale=0.25), Entropy-regularized categorical outcome quantiles (bonus=0.01) over active observed prototypes per context; associative fast-weight extrapolation disabled (assoc_ridge=0, assoc_max_blend=0), Enable detached Sherman-Morrison recursive associative fast-weight operator (assoc_ridge=0.05, assoc_forgetting=1.0, assoc_blend_scale=0.25, assoc_max_blend=1.0) to extrapolate latent feature expectations smoothly beyond discrete context tolerance

### `online-gail-bcap-momentum` (`GAIL-BCAP`)
- **Changed factors**: ParticleGAN 0.9.6 public bcap_adam trainer preset, Shared 64-frame FIFO memory, seven replay samples and velocity context, Global non-saturating loss and cosine schedule (start=0.5, floor=0.05), Explicit replay_policy=none: train every arriving observation once, batch_size=1, Predict before arrival; learn next-frame targets from the preceding detached context, Causal state reader queries detached associative memory and carries current image, velocity, salient history and bounded repetition age in the existing 16-dimensional context, New observed-feature conditional prior: fixed named deterministic orthogonal 8x64 encoder; at most 32 latent feature means and 64 observed-context outcome counts; learn statistics only after successful current-observation updates, Freeze storage prior; learned additive conditional transform preserves each sampled MoG perturbation and task capacity, samples empirical feature quantiles at nearest past context; no image output bypass or stored-frame replay, G-phase posterior encodes only the current arrived frame; BCE reconstruction weight 10 trains the unchanged image decoder; D and serving use learned conditional prior without current target or generator probes, Ablate associative-history context term; retain appearance, velocity, salient content and repetition age, Switch critic adversarial objective to Hinge loss paired with BCAP input-gradient cap and write_lr=0.5 causal-state fast-weight updates, Stabilized GAIL-BCAP generator objective: cancel unbounded linear Hinge generator slope and optimize prioritized BCE survival anchor minus bounded sigmoid critic style bonus (weight 0.1) modulated by causal discounted return advantage (gamma=0.99, scale=0.25), Entropy-regularized categorical outcome quantiles (bonus=0.01) over active observed prototypes per context; associative fast-weight extrapolation disabled (assoc_ridge=0, assoc_max_blend=0), Enable detached Sherman-Morrison recursive associative fast-weight operator (assoc_ridge=0.05, assoc_forgetting=1.0, assoc_blend_scale=0.25, assoc_max_blend=1.0) to extrapolate latent feature expectations smoothly beyond discrete context tolerance, Enable nonzero Adam first-moment smoothing (betas=[0.5, 0.999]) across sequential batch_size=1 arrivals

at most 32 latent feature means and 64 observed-context outcome counts; learn statistics only after successful current-observation updates, Freeze storage prior; learned additive conditional transform preserves each sampled MoG perturbation and task capacity, samples empirical feature quantiles at nearest past context; no image output bypass or stored-frame replay, G-phase posterior encodes only the current arrived frame; BCE reconstruction weight 10 trains the unchanged image decoder; D and serving use learned conditional prior without current target or generator probes, Ablate associative-history context term; retain appearance, velocity, salient content and repetition age, Switch critic adversarial objective to Hinge loss paired with BCAP input-gradient cap and write_lr=0.5 causal-state fast-weight updates, Stabilized GAIL-BCAP generator objective: cancel unbounded linear Hinge generator slope and optimize prioritized BCE survival anchor minus bounded sigmoid critic style bonus (weight 0.1) modulated by causal discounted return advantage (gamma=0.99, scale=0.25), Entropy-regularized categorical outcome quantiles (bonus=0.01) over active observed prototypes per context; - **Changed factors**: ParticleGAN 0.9.6 public bcap_adam trainer preset, Shared 64-frame FIFO memory, seven replay samples and velocity context, Global non-saturating loss and cosine schedule (start=0.5, floor=0.05), Explicit replay_policy=none: train every arriving observation once, batch_size=1, Predict before arrival; learn next-frame targets from the preceding detached context, Causal state reader queries detached associative memory and carries current image, velocity, salient history and bounded repetition age in the existing 16-dimensional context, New observed-feature conditional prior: fixed named deterministic orthogonal 8x64 encoder; at most 32 latent feature means and 64 observed-context outcome counts; learn statistics only after successful current-observation updates, Freeze storage prior; learned additive conditional transform preserves each sampled MoG perturbation and task capacity, samples empirical feature quantiles at nearest past context; no image output bypass or stored-frame replay, G-phase posterior encodes only the current arrived frame; BCE reconstruction weight 10 trains the unchanged image decoder; D and serving use learned conditional prior without current target or generator probes, Ablate associative-history context term; retain appearance, velocity, salient content and repetition age, Switch critic adversarial objective to Hinge loss paired with BCAP input-gradient cap and write_lr=0.5 causal-state fast-weight updates, Stabilized GAIL-BCAP generator objective: cancel unbounded linear Hinge generator slope and optimize prioritized BCE survival anchor minus bounded sigmoid critic style bonus (weight 0.1) modulated by causal discounted return advantage (gamma=0.99, scale=0.25), Entropy-regularized categorical outcome quantiles (bonus=0.01) over active observed prototypes per context; associative fast-weight extrapolation disabled (assoc_ridge=0, assoc_max_blend=0), Enable detached Sherman-Morrison recursive associative fast-weight operator (assoc_ridge=0.05, assoc_forgetting=1.0, assoc_blend_scale=0.25, assoc_max_blend=1.0) to extrapolate latent feature expectations smoothly beyond discrete context tolerance, Replace radial distance blending with float64 Sherman-Morrison RLS (assoc_ridge=0.1) + precision-weighted Mahalanobis spike-and-slab prototype transport (spike_slab_scale=1.0) and empirical-Bayes kernel-gated active-entropy residual quantiles, Add Markov context-bypass regularizer (context_bypass_weight=5.0), calibrated sigmoid style bonus (style_bonus_weight=0.05), and unnormalized linear causal key projection (normalize_key=false, key_gain=0.5) preserving Euclidean superposition norm

### `online-subspace-prior` (`SubspacePrior-BCAP`)
- **Changed factors**: ParticleGAN 0.9.6 public bcap_adam trainer preset, Shared 64-frame FIFO memory, seven replay samples and velocity context, Global non-saturating loss and cosine schedule (start=0.5, floor=0.05), Explicit replay_policy=none: train every arriving observation once, batch_size=1, Predict before arrival; learn next-frame targets from the preceding detached context, Causal state reader queries detached associative memory and carries current image, velocity, salient history and bounded repetition age in the existing 16-dimensional context, New observed-feature conditional prior: fixed named deterministic orthogonal 8x64 encoder; at most 32 latent feature means and 64 observed-context outcome counts; learn statistics only after successful current-observation updates, Freeze storage prior; learned additive conditional transform preserves each sampled MoG perturbation and task capacity, samples empirical feature quantiles at nearest past context; no image output bypass or stored-frame replay, G-phase posterior encodes only the current arrived frame; BCE reconstruction weight 10 trains the unchanged image decoder; D and serving use learned conditional prior without current target or generator probes, Ablate associative-history context term; retain appearance, velocity, salient content and repetition age, Parametric residual conditional prior: online float64 Sherman-Morrison RLS affine predictor plus Mahalanobis precision spike-and-slab prototype transport and empirical-Bayes kernel-gated live residual quantiles replace 1-NN context lookup on unseen states, Markov bottleneck regularizer (context_bypass_weight=5.0) penalizes direct generator input-context shortcut weights so held-out predictions decode through the conditional latent prior, Unnormalized salient causal key projection (normalize_key=false, key_gain=0.5) preserves Euclidean superposition norm across compositional and continuous kinematic inputs while retaining contrast gating, Precision-whitened anchored Gram-Schmidt innovation subspace (subspace_dims=6, abs_tol=0.008, rel_tol=0.12, nu_0=0.10, feature_scale=2.1) plus 2-row deterministic JL tail eliminates blind 8x64 projection nullspace and 1:1 MoG Wiener attenuation without retroactive coordinate drift, Semiparametric continuous Nadaraya-Watson kernel-ridge residual (nw_weight=1.0, nw_bandwidth=0.65, nw_ridge=0.06) over unquantized per-bucket latent means gated by (1 - g_disc) captures local nonlinear dynamics alongside discrete quantiles, Single-arrival critic calming and posterior smoothing (d_lr_mult=0.02, reconstruction_weight=100.0, betas=[0.4, 0.999], lr=0.007, lr_anneal_start=0.55, lr_floor=0.06)

### `online-bcap-transport` (`BCAP`)
- **Changed factors**: ParticleGAN 0.9.6 public bcap_adam trainer preset, Shared 64-frame FIFO memory, seven replay samples and velocity context, Global non-saturating loss and cosine schedule (start=0.5, floor=0.05), Explicit replay_policy=none: train every arriving observation once, batch_size=1, Predict before arrival; learn next-frame targets from the preceding detached context, Public prior manipulator: uniform critic sampling; generator routes the current observation by sequential clean-center MSE plus 0.01 log row usage, adds weight-10 reconstruction on one generated image, and clears the target before serving
